L576: [tool_result] export class DuckDbOp extends Operator<DuckDbOp> {
L577:   static displayName = 'DuckDB'
L578:   static description = 'Query a DuckDB database using sql'
L579:   asDownload = () => this.outputData
L580:   createInputs() {
L581:     return {
L582:       query: new CodeField('SELECT 1 as val;', { language: 'sql' }),
L583:     }
L584:   }
L585:   createOutputs() {
L586:     return {
L587:       data: new DataField(),
L588:     }
L589:   }
L590: 
L591:   async execute({
L592:     query: queryString = '',
L593:   }: ExtractProps<typeof this.inputs>): ExtractProps<typeof this.outputs> | null {
L594:     const queries = queryString
L595:       .split(';')
L596:       .map(s => s.trim())
L597:       .filter(Boolean)
L598:       .map(s => `${s};`)
L599:     if (!queries?.length) {
L600:       return { data: [] }
L601:     }
L602: 
L603:     const db = await duckDbInstance
L604:     const conn = await db.connect()
L605: 
L606:     try {
L607:       let data = []
L608:       for (const query of queries) {
L609:         if (!mustacheRe.test(query)) {
L610:           const result = await conn.query(query)
L611:           data = result.toArray()
L612:           continue
L613:         }
L614: 
L615:         // Parse the query and extract references
L616:         const references: FieldReference[] = []
L617:         const parameterizedQuery = query.replace(mustacheRe, (raw, opId, inOut, fieldPath) => {
L618:           // If the opId is a relative path (doesn't start with /), make it relative to current context
L619:           const resolvedOpId = opId.startsWith('/') ? opId : `./${opId}`
L620:           references.push({ opId: resolvedOpId, inOut, fieldPath, raw })
L621:           return `$${references.length}` // $1, $2, etc.
L622:         })
L623: 
L624:         // Resolve reference values
L625:         const positionalParams = references.map(({ opId, inOut, fieldPath }) => {
L626:           const op = getOp(opId, this.id)
L627:           const [firstKey, ...rest] = fieldPath.split('.')
L628: 
L629:           const field = op?.[inOut === 'par' ? 'inputs' : 'outputs']?.[firstKey]
L630:           if (!field) {
L631:             throw new Error(`Field ${firstKey} not found on ${opId}`)
L632:           }
L633: 
L634:           return rest.reduce((d, prop) => d[prop], field.value)
L635:         })
L636: 
L637:         // Prepare the query with the current connection
L638:         const prepared = await conn.prepare(parameterizedQuery)
L639: 
L640:         const result = await prepared.query(...positionalParams)
L641:         data = result.toArray()
L642:       }
L643:       await conn.close()
L644:       return { data }
L645:     } catch (e) {
L646:       debugExecute('Error executing query', e)
L647:       await conn.close()
L648:       await db.reset()
L649:       if (e instanceof Error) {
L650:         throw e
L651:       }
L652:       return null
L653:     }
L654:   }
L655: }

L1159: function contextGeneratorPlugin() {
L1160:   let isGenerating = false
L1161:   let needsRegeneration = false
L1162: 
L1163:   const watchedPaths = [
L1164:     'src/noodles/operators.ts',
L1165:     'src/noodles/fields.ts',
L1166:     'src/noodles/components/categories.ts',
L1167:     'src/ai-chat/**/*.md',
L1168:     'src/examples/**/noodles.json',
L1169:     'src/examples/**/README.md',
L1170:   ]
L1171: 
L1172:   async function generateContext() {
L1173:     if (isGenerating) {
L1174:       needsRegeneration = true
L1175:       return
L1176:     }
L1177: 
L1178:     isGenerating = true
L1179:     needsRegeneration = false
L1180: 
L1181:     try {
L1182:       console.log('\n🔄 Regenerating AI context bundles...')
L1183:       execSync('npm run generate:context', {
L1184:         stdio: 'inherit',
L1185:         cwd: process.cwd(),
L1186:       })
L1187:       console.log('✅ AI context bundles updated\n')
L1188:     } catch (error) {
L1189:       console.error('❌ Failed to generate context:', error.message)
L1190:     } finally {
L1191:       isGenerating = false
L1192: 
L1193:       // If files changed while we were generating, trigger another generation
L1194:       if (needsRegeneration) {
L1195:         setTimeout(() => generateContext(), 100)
L1196:       }
L1197:     }
L1198:   }
L1199: 
L1200:   return {
L1201:     name: 'context-generator',
L1202:     apply: 'serve', // Only run in dev mode
L1203:     configureServer(server) {
L1204:       // Generate context on server start
L1205:       generateContext()
L1206: 
L1207:       // Watch for file changes
L1208:       server.watcher.on('change', file => {
L1209:         const relativePath = path.relative(server.config.root, file)
L1210: 
L1211:         // Check if the changed file matches any watched patterns
L1212:         const shouldRegenerate = watchedPaths.some(pattern => {
L1213:           if (pattern.includes('**')) {
L1214:             const regex = new RegExp(`^${pattern.replace(/\*\*/g, '.*').replace(/\*/g, '[^/]*')}$`)
L1215:             return regex.test(relativePath)
L1216:           }
L1217:           return relativePath === pattern
L1218:         })
L1219: 
L1220:         if (shouldRegenerate) {
L1221:           console.log(`📝 Detected change in ${relativePath}`)
L1222:           generateContext()
L1223:         }
L1224:       })
L1225:     },
L1226:   }
L1227: }

L1514: [assistant] Now I understand the full picture. The CSV is in `src/examples/quake-hexbins/data.csv`. The project will use `@/data.csv` as the path. Now let me understand how DuckDB reads these files — it uses the `FileOp` + `DuckDbOp` pattern. For DuckDB SQL with the `@/` path, the approach is to use `FileOp` to load data then `DuckDbOp` references that via `op()`, OR to use `read_csv` inside DuckDB pointing to a fetched URL.

L1516: Let me check how storage resolves data for DuckDB queries:
L1517: [tool_use Bash] {"command":"sed -n '2155,2230p' /tmp/noodles-evals/workspaces/sql-h3-pipeline--claude-sonnet-4-6--s3/noodles-editor/src/noodles/operators.ts","description":"Read DuckDbOp execute method"}
L1518: [tool_result]       return { data: [] }
L1519:     }
L1520: 
L1521:     const db = await duckDbInstance
L1522:     const conn = await db.connect()
L1523: 
L1524:     try {
L1525:       let data = []
L1526:       for (const query of queries) {
L1527:         if (!mustacheRe.test(query)) {
L1528:           const result = await conn.query(query)
L1529:           data = result.toArray()
L1530:           continue
L1531:         }
L1532: 
L1533:         // Parse the query and extract references
L1534:         const references: FieldReference[] = []
L1535:         const parameterizedQuery = query.replace(mustacheRe, (raw, opId, inOut, fieldPath) => {
L1536:           // If the opId is a relative path (doesn't start with /), make it relative to current context
L1537:           const resolvedOpId = opId.startsWith('/') ? opId : `./${opId}`
L1538:           references.push({ opId: resolvedOpId, inOut, fieldPath, raw })
L1539:           return `$${references.length}` // $1, $2, etc.
L1540:         })
L1541: 
L1542:         // Resolve reference values
L1543:         const positionalParams = references.map(({ opId, inOut, fieldPath }) => {
L1544:           const op = getOp(opId, this.id)
L1545:           const [firstKey, ...rest] = fieldPath.split('.')
L1546: 
L1547:           const field = op?.[inOut === 'par' ? 'inputs' : 'outputs']?.[firstKey]
L1548:           if (!field) {
L1549:             throw new Error(`Field ${firstKey} not found on ${opId}`)
L1550:           }
L1551: 
L1552:           return rest.reduce((d, prop) => d[prop], field.value)
L1553:         })
L1554: 
L1555:         // Prepare the query with the current connection
L1556:         const prepared = await conn.prepare(parameterizedQuery)
L1557: 
L1558:         const result = await prepared.query(...positionalParams)
L1559:         data = result.toArray()
L1560:       }
L1561:       await conn.close()
L1562:       return { data }
L1563:     } catch (e) {
L1564:       debugExecute('Error executing query', e)
L1565:       await conn.close()
L1566:       await db.reset()
L1567:       if (e instanceof Error) {
L1568:         throw e
L1569:       }
L1570:       return null
L1571:     }
L1572:   }

L11442: 
L11443: 1. Use `FileOp` (with `@/data.csv`) to load the CSV data
